API - mc3s-b2x-productcatalog

mc3s-b2x-productcatalog REST API [build:2024-11-14T00:36:24.118Z]

Header

The application accepts header parameters to control context

| Header | Description | | ------------------ | --------------- | | Content-Language | Active language | | X-Store | Active store | | X-Organization | Active Unit | | X-Company | Active Company | | X-AnonymousId | Anonymous Id |

Key Features 'mc3s-b2x-productcatalog'

Category

APIs to fetch getting category information

  • Get category tree for store
  • get category details
  • get category hierarchy

Product

APIs to fetch product data

  • Get product by ID
  • Get product by Key
  • Get product by Sku

ProductInStore

APIs to fetch product data for current store

  • Get product by ID
  • Get product by Key
  • Get product by Sku

Product Types

APIs to fetch Product Type data

  • Get Product Type by ID
  • Get Product Type by Key
  • Get Product Type Meta Data by ID
  • Get Product Type Meta Data by Key

Listing

APIs to fetch product listing information

  • Product Listing for store by ID
  • Product Listing for store by Key
  • Product Listing for store by SKU
  • Product Listing for store by cart
  • Product Listing for store by order
  • Product Listing for store by shoppinglist

AttributeGroup

APIs to fetch Attribute Groups

  • Get all Attribute Groups
  • Get Attribute Group by ID
  • Get Attribute Group by Key
  • Get Attribute Group for Product Type
More information: https://mindcurvgroup.com/en/services/digital-experiences-solutions/b2xaccelerator-for-composable-platforms-built-with-mach.html
Contact Info: info@mindcurv.com

AttributeGroup

Up 
get /aggregator/attribute-group/{id}
(attributeGroupById)
AttributeGroup by id (scope: catalog).

Path parameters

id (required)
Path Parameter — AttributeGroup id default: null

Return type

AttributeGroupAggregation

Example data

{

  "description" : "description",
  "attributes" : [ "attributes", "attributes" ],
  "id" : "id",
  "key" : "key"
}

get /aggregator/category
(categories)

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

200

Success PageableOfCategoryAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/category/{id}
(categoryById)
Category by id (scope: not required).

Path parameters

id (required)
Path Parameter — Category id default: null

Return type

CategoryAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found CategoryAggregation

200

Success CategoryAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/category/key={key}
(categoryByKey)
Category by key (scope: not required).

Path parameters

key (required)
Path Parameter — Category key default: null

Return type

CategoryAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found CategoryAggregation

200

Success CategoryAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/category/slug={slug}
(categoryBySlug)
Category by slug (scope: not required).

Path parameters

slug (required)
Path Parameter — Category slug default: null

Return type

CategoryAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product/key={key}
(productByKey)
Product by key (scope: not required).

Path parameters

key (required)
Path Parameter — Product key default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product/sku={sku}
(productBySku)
Product by sku (scope: not required).

Path parameters

sku (required)
Path Parameter — Sku id default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product/slug={slug}
(productBySlug)
Product by slug (scope: not required).

Path parameters

slug (required)
Path Parameter — Product slug default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product
(products)
All products (scope: not required).

Query parameters

offset (optional)
Query Parameter — Page index default: 0 format: int64
limit (optional)
Query Parameter — Items per page default: 20 format: int64

Return type

PageableOfProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

200

Success PageableOfProductAggregation

ProductInStore

Up 
get /aggregator/product-store/{id}
(productInStoreById)
Product in store by id (scope: not required).

Path parameters

id (required)
Path Parameter — Product id default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product-store/key={key}
(productInStoreByKey)
Product in store by key (scope: not required).

Path parameters

key (required)
Path Parameter — Product key default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product-store/sku={sku}
(productInStoreBySku)
Product in store by sku (scope: not required).

Path parameters

sku (required)
Path Parameter — Sku default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation

Produces

This API call produces the following media types according to the Accept request header; the media type will be conveyed by the Content-Type response header.
  • application/json

Responses

404

Not found ProductAggregation

200

Success ProductAggregation
Up 
get /aggregator/product-store/slug={slug}
(productInStoreBySlug)
Product in store by slug (scope: not required).

Path parameters

slug (required)
Path Parameter — slug default: null

Return type

ProductAggregation
